Without explicit handling, any consumer that called * `onCmpResolved` after the publisher (`conveyFidesCmpResolution`) had already dispatched the * `FIDES_CMP_RESOLVED` event would hang forever waiting for an event * that already happened. * * - **Late subscriber** (`lastCmpResolution !== undefined`): the resolution * has already been published. The last few titles on the list known as the extended list, never have arrows.</p></li><li class="css-1d9bvr1"><p class="css-2yw3mw">An asterisk indicates that a book&#x27;s sales are barely distinguishable from those of the book above it.</p></li><li class="css-1mn6y7f"><p class="css-2yw3mw">A dagger indicates that some retailers report receiving bulk orders.</p></li></ul></aside></div><div class="css-1v9qjcu"><aside><p class="css-nn2hcj"><em>Follow New York Times Books on </em><a class="css-19icsj4" href="https://www.facebook.com/nytbooks/" title="" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank"><em>Facebook</em></a><em> and </em><a class="css-19icsj4" href="https://twitter.com/nytimesbooks" title="" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank"><em>Twitter (@nytimesbooks)</em></a><em>, sign up for </em><a class="css-19icsj4" href="https://www.nytimes.com/newsletters/books-review" title=""><em>our newsletter</em></a><em> or </em><a class="css-19icsj4" href="https://www.nytimes.com/interactive/2017/books/books-calendar.html" title=""><em>our literary calendar</em></a><em>.
